Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Theorem 221.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in diff_geom theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 33 and the invariant I(A,B) = 267 mod 3.

Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
